# MADAM BORROW-BORROW: Video Prompts

274 video prompts, one for each image prompt. Animate each from its matching still image. Duration equals the beat length, rounded up, minimum 3 s.

## Beat 1 (3s)
**[If borrowing were a profession,]**

**Video Prompt:** Stylized 3D CGI animation in Pixar/Disney feature-film style, animated from the matching still. Duration: 3 seconds. Scene: Ifeoma strutting proudly across the compound yard, balancing a borrowed aluminium pot on her head, a folded lace wrapper over one arm and a blue plastic chair in the other hand, beaming with confidence. Characters: Ifeoma, a plump, curvy Igbo woman in her early thirties with rich brown skin, a round cheerful face, big expressive eyes and a wide charming smile, wearing an orange-and-teal ankara wrapper with a matching puffed-sleeve blouse, a teal headwrap and small gold hoop earrings. Setting: a Nigerian village compound in Umuoji with a red-earth yard, cream-painted zinc-roof bungalows, banana trees, a clothesline and plastic buckets. Character motion: Strutting across the yard balancing borrowed items, performed with exaggerated, readable Pixar-style body language; a sly sideways glance, a slow growing grin. Environment motion: banana leaves swaying gently, clothes fluttering on the line, a chicken pecking in the background, faint dust drifting in the sunlight. Camera movement: slow, subtle dolly-in, starting from a medium-wide, eye level framing. Lighting: warm late-morning sunlight from the side, soft shadows, gentle rim light, light atmospheric haze, consistent throughout the shot. Mood: playful, mischievous. Smooth 24fps cinematic motion, consistent character design and outfits, natural physics, no morphing, no flicker, no text, no watermark, no subtitles.

- **Duration:** 3s
- **Camera Movement:** Slow, subtle dolly-in
- **Character Motion:** Strutting across the yard balancing borrowed items, performed with exaggerated, readable Pixar-style body language; a sly sideways glance, a slow growing grin
- **Environment Motion:** Banana leaves swaying gently, clothes fluttering on the line, a chicken pecking in the background, faint dust drifting in the sunlight
